# Sands of the Duat Art Bible

These 161 creature entries cover the owner-reviewed cast and the Wave B/C/D1/D2/D3
mechanic families. The binding Sands of the Duat register in `docs/art-bible/index.md`
governs every palette, value floor, lighting pair, blank cartouche, species tell,
and mechanic-family composition below. Card facts are transcribed from the landed
set data in source-file order.

### Bastet, Mistress of the Ninth Return — `sd-bastet-mistress-of-the-ninth-return`
- **Card facts:** {3}{R}{W} · W/R (gold frame) · 5/4 · twinBlades, warcry · ur, legendary · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** Adult woman and named Bastet legend, the lioness-eared warrior queen who founded the tribe's return. Keep the real deity name only on this legend entry. Her body is tall and powerfully athletic with warm copper-brown skin, broad shoulders, a narrow waist, and a battle-ready adult presence.
- **Personality / mood:** Mythic authority after surviving the road eight times; still, dry, and unafraid. The image carries the weight of a queen who has learned what the Duat asks, not a comic adventurer.
- **Pose & composition:** Second-landing Nine Lives beat, not a death: one hand planted on the Lion Gate flagstone as her weight transfers forward from one knee, dust and grit still falling, with eight scorched outlines of her prior landings behind her. The canonical life mark is a notch in her left ear with one plain gold ring through it. Face and eye line sit around y 330 inside the safe band y 200–560; the gate, landing trace, and gold mark remain in the central band y 167–633. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q5):** the rarity badge is vetoed; collar rows are the artist's choice. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q6):** the roughly one-third daylight share stands.
- **Costume & attire:** Low weighted braid and no towering headdress; two upright tufted ears with short tufts set high on the skull; exactly ONE unforked tail with exactly ONE tip emerging from the base of the spine at the tailbone, centered on the lower back, never from the hip, side, or waist; slit vertical amber irises with narrow vertical slit pupils; wrapped ankles and bare soles. A hard triangular Bastet silhouette uses a broad gold-and-lapis collar, bare midriff and forearms, pleated linen wrap-kilt, bronze bracers, and a leather blade harness. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q5):** the rarity badge is vetoed; collar rows are the artist's choice.
- **Palette:** W/R identity anchor: W `#f2e8cf`, `#c9a84c`, `#fffef2` layered with R `#d95436`, `#5e0f0f`, `#f7b267`; Duat accents tomb gold `#e8c95a` / `#c9a84c`, lapis `#1f3f7a` / `#2f5fae`, sandstone `#d8b98a` / `#a8834f`, and barge fire `#ff8a3d` / `#ffd07a` in the noon warmth. Heart-red `#c8253a` is reserved and absent here. Value floor: no true black; the darkest visible value is resin brown `#3a2a1c`, with lapis shadow providing depth.
- **Lighting:** Named practical key: the visible hard noon sun disc above the Lion Gate, striking her face, collar, and landing hand. One lapis-cool rim from blue inlay in the gate and the heat-hazed sky; no second key or unmotivated ambient source.
- **Expression:** A fierce, composed adult woman's half-smile, eyes forward and alert, with the confidence of a return already earned. No coyness, no comedy, no open-mouth fangs required.
- **Props / weapon:** Her planted hand, gold ear ring, and eight scorched landing outlines are the mechanical evidence. A short khopesh remains secured at her hip rather than stealing the second-landing read; a broad, empty cartouche shield lies behind her as geometric gold and lapis panels.
- **Background:** Fully rendered noon Lion Gate environment: white sandstone threshold, hard short shadows, heat shimmer, worn flagstones, and the eight faint scorched silhouettes receding toward the gate. Every wall register and cartouche is a blank gold or lapis panel with geometric banding, never a glyph.
- **Holo interaction:** Shiny highlight catches the warm face-and-collar light while lapis rim separation stays readable at battlefield crop.
- **Rarity ambition:** UR legend intent: full environmental splash, clean silhouette, generous headroom, and a thumbnail-readable gold life mark. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q5):** the rarity badge is vetoed; collar rows are the artist's choice. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q6):** the roughly one-third daylight share stands.
- **Prompt:** Adult woman Bastet warrior queen in a higher second landing at the mouth of a monumental Lion Gate at hard noon, weight rising forward from one knee with her face raised, MUST show: narrow cat-slit vertical pupils like a cat eye, never round pupils, two upright tufted ears, exactly ONE unforked tail with its base VISIBLE emerging at the tailbone center of the lower back; her long tail curls UP and around beside her waist with its full arc clearly visible at waist height at mid-frame, never hanging low near the ground; amber-gold eyes, wrapped ankles and bare soles, low weighted braid, broad gold-and-lapis collar, athletic warm copper-brown adult body, several scorched outlines of her prior landings on the flagstones around her at mid-frame, all eight landing outlines at mid-height in the center of the frame, fully visible in the upper two thirds, never near the bottom edge, canonical life mark as a notch in one ear with one plain gold ring at mid-height in the center of the frame, fully visible in the upper two thirds, never near the bottom edge, visible noon sun disc placed lower in the frame at mid-height in the center of the frame, fully visible in the upper two thirds, never near the bottom edge, W/R identity palette with Duat tomb gold, lapis, sandstone, and barge-fire accents, visible noon sun as practical key and one lapis-cool inlay rim, a generous pool of warm light claiming a large part of the frame, rich readable detail in every shadow, no near-black areas anywhere, blank gold cartouche panels, geometric banded registers, all distant walls, pylons, columns and background architecture perfectly smooth blank stone with plain flat geometric banding only, no carved relief anywhere, NO-TEXT, no hieroglyphs, no glyphs, no writing, no inscriptions, no true black, darkest visible value resin brown `#3a2a1c`, her head fully inside the middle band of the frame, clear air above it with generous empty air above the head, fully rendered sandstone gate and heat-shimmer background — crisp cel-shaded gacha anime splash art, fully rendered scenic background, 640×800 portrait

### Anubis, Who Holds the Scale — `sd-anubis-who-holds-the-scale`
- **Card facts:** {5}{W}{B} · W/B (gold frame) · 7/7 · sentinel · ur, legendary · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** Adult woman and named Anubis legend, the jackal-eared judge who holds the beam level without deciding the verdict. Her build is tall, columnar, and statuesque with deep umber skin and a calm, unmistakably adult presence.
- **Personality / mood:** Immoveable truth, solemn patience, and the quiet pressure of a judgment that cannot be bargained with. Mythic weight only; no office or institutional comedy.
- **Pose & composition:** Preserve scene showing the copy, not a corpse: two identical Anubis silhouettes occupy the same axis, the original low in resin-brown shadow and the token copy standing in natron white and gold where the original knelt. Both hands are off the dead-level scale, whose beam balances a feather against a weighed heart. Face and eye line sit around y 340; the two light states, beam, feather, and heart all remain in y 167–633. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q4):** the heart-red reservation is confirmed and binding.
- **Costume & attire:** White linen sheath with broad gold collar, jackal ears and a long deep resin-brown jackal muzzle rendered in cel planes, gold wrist cuffs, and a measured columnar silhouette. The copy repeats every shape in clean natron white; no rot, exposed bone, flies, or green skin.
- **Palette:** W/B identity anchor: W `#f2e8cf`, `#c9a84c`, `#fffef2` against B `#5a3a70`, `#140d1c`, `#9b6fc4`, with Duat tomb gold `#e8c95a` / `#c9a84c`, lapis `#1f3f7a` / `#2f5fae`, sandstone `#d8b98a` / `#a8834f`, natron white `#f4efe2`, and resin brown `#4a2f16`. Heart-red `#c8253a` appears only on the single weighed heart. Value floor: no true black; darkest visible value is resin brown `#3a2a1c`, with lapis shadow instead of black.
- **Lighting:** Named practical key: a low oil brazier visible beside the scale, warming the natron copy, beam, and Anubis's gold hands. One lapis-cool rim from blue inlay along the colonnade and the copy's far edge; no extra light source.
- **Expression:** Neutral, level, and severe without anger; the adult woman looks at the balance rather than the viewer, as if the beam is the only testimony she needs.
- **Props / weapon:** Empty hands held just clear of the beam to make the refusal to interfere visible; one gold scale, one pale feather, and one heart in the pan. The heart is the only saturated cool red; there is no weapon and no payoff shown.
- **Background:** A fully rendered alabaster judgment hall receding into resin-brown colonnades, with the original silhouette low in shadow and the natron-white copy occupying the same kneeling space. Wall registers, seals, and cartouches are blank gold or lapis panels with geometric banding, never glyphs.
- **Holo interaction:** Shiny highlight catches the level beam and natron copy while the reserved heart-red remains stable and isolated.
- **Rarity ambition:** UR legend intent: symmetrical judgment tableau, precise beam geometry, and two-value Preserve read at thumbnail size. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q4):** the heart-red reservation is confirmed and binding.
- **Prompt:** Adult woman Anubis, jackal-eared judge in white linen and gold, EXACTLY TWO figures in the entire image and not one person more, a Preserve composition of the same woman twice: the original kneeling low in resin-brown shadow and the clean token copy standing where the original knelt (two heights) in natron white and gold, a CLOSE waist-up composition, she holds the balance scale RAISED HIGH beside her face with the beam at the height of her eyes, both pans hanging on short chains at her chest, the feather in one pan and the small heart-red weighed heart in the other pan both large and clearly visible right beside her face in the upper half of the frame, solemn umber face, W/B identity palette with tomb gold, lapis, sandstone, natron white, and resin-brown accents, low oil brazier inside the frame as the practical key and one lapis-cool rim from inlaid colonnade, a generous pool of warm light claiming a large part of the frame, rich readable detail in every shadow, no near-black areas anywhere, blank gold cartouche panels, geometric banded registers, all distant walls, pylons, columns and background architecture perfectly smooth blank stone with plain flat geometric banding only, no carved relief anywhere, NO-TEXT, no hieroglyphs, no glyphs, no writing, no inscriptions, no rot, no exposed bone, no flies, no true black, darkest visible value resin brown `#3a2a1c`, no saturated red except the single weighed heart, her head fully inside the middle band of the frame, clear air above it with generous empty air above the head, fully rendered alabaster judgment hall — crisp cel-shaded gacha anime splash art, fully rendered scenic background, 640×800 portrait

### Osiris, Green After Burial — `sd-osiris-green-after-burial`
- **Card facts:** {4}{B}{G} · B/G (gold frame) · 6/6 · ur, legendary · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** Adult woman and named Osiris legend, the first return whose malachite skin is an honor rather than a wound. She is a tall, broad-shouldered adult queen with saturated malachite-green skin, a long torso, and a grounded, powerful stance.
- **Personality / mood:** Returned without apology, practical and patient; the crop reads as renewal with grave authority, not decay. Her calm is older than the broken sarcophagus beneath her.
- **Pose & composition:** Dual Preserve and Nine Lives composition: the original Osiris is low in resin-brown shadow beside the broken sarcophagus lid while a clean natron-white copy occupies that space in a clear second-landing beat, one foot transferring forward and silt still falling. The canonical life mark is a thin gold seam across the original's upper arm where the wound was; a cracked lid and fresh silt outline mark where she went down. Face and eye line sit around y 320, and both copies remain legible in the central band.
- **Costume & attire:** Bright natron linen wraps chest-down, bare forearms, low braided hair under a restrained royal band, broad gold-and-lapis collar, and a crook and flail held like tools rather than regalia. The copy repeats the clean linen and gold; the malachite skin is exclusive to Osiris and is never used as a generic green shadow.
- **Palette:** B/G identity anchor: B `#5a3a70`, `#140d1c`, `#9b6fc4` layered with G `#4fa06a`, `#123a22`, `#a9dcae`; Duat accents tomb gold `#e8c95a` / `#c9a84c`, lapis `#1f3f7a` / `#2f5fae`, sandstone `#d8b98a` / `#a8834f`, natron white `#f4efe2`, resin brown `#4a2f16`, and malachite `#2f8f6a` only for her skin and jewelry inlay. Heart-red `#c8253a` is absent. Value floor: no true black; darkest visible value is resin brown `#3a2a1c`.
- **Lighting:** Named practical key: a high sun shaft through a broken tomb roof, striking the natron-white copy and barley heads. One lapis-cool rim from floodwater and blue stone inlay separates both silhouettes; no unmotivated ambient or second key.
- **Expression:** A serene, unsmiling adult queen with eyes lifted toward the new growth, carrying the knowledge that return is work.
- **Props / weapon:** Crook and flail crossed over the chest as practical tools, broken sarcophagus lid, fresh silt, and the thin gold seam life mark. The props support both the copy and second-landing reads without showing a corpse.
- **Background:** A fully rendered barley field growing directly from a broken sarcophagus lid, with tomb masonry receding into resin-brown shade and flood-green shoots in the central band. Blank gold cartouche panels and geometric banded registers replace every possible inscription.
- **Holo interaction:** Shiny highlight favors the clean Preserve copy's natron-white light while the gold seam remains readable across the original.
- **Rarity ambition:** UR legend intent: full environmental richness, high-contrast twin silhouette, and a clear Preserve copy plus Nine Lives landing story without gore.
- **Prompt:** Adult woman Osiris, tall broad-shouldered malachite-green queen wrapped chest-down in bright natron linen, exactly two identical figures and no other person in a dual Preserve and Nine Lives scene, the original low in resin-brown shadow beside a broken sarcophagus lid and the clean natron-white token copy making a second landing into the same space, silt falling, the copy and thin gold-seam life mark clearly visible at mid-frame, both at mid-height in the center of the frame, fully visible in the upper two thirds, never near the bottom edge, fresh cracked silt outline showing where she went down at mid-height in the center of the frame, fully visible in the upper two thirds, never near the bottom edge, crook and flail crossed over the chest, B/G identity palette with Duat tomb gold, lapis, sandstone, natron white, resin brown, malachite skin only, high roof-slot sun shaft as practical key and one lapis-cool floodwater rim, a generous pool of warm light claiming a large part of the frame, rich readable detail in every shadow, no near-black areas anywhere, blank gold cartouche panels, geometric banded registers, all distant walls, pylons, columns and background architecture perfectly smooth blank stone with plain flat geometric banding only, no carved relief anywhere, NO-TEXT, no hieroglyphs, no glyphs, no writing, no inscriptions, no rot, no exposed bone, no flies, no true black, darkest visible value resin brown `#3a2a1c`, her head fully inside the middle band of the frame, clear air above it with generous empty air above the head, fully rendered barley field and tomb background — crisp cel-shaded gacha anime splash art, fully rendered scenic background, 640×800 portrait

### Ammit, Under the Scale — `sd-ammit-under-the-scale`
- **Card facts:** {5}{B}{R} · B/R (gold frame) · 10/8 · dreaded, warcry · ur, legendary · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** Adult woman and named Ammit legend, a composite predator woman whose crocodile jaw and lion mane define the silhouette without making her a monster prop. She is low, massively built, and adult, with tawny-brown skin, a powerful crouch, and controlled predatory weight.
- **Personality / mood:** Patient inevitability beneath the beam. The dread is a price honestly paid, never a joke and never a killing spectacle.
- **Pose & composition:** Rite scene shows the payment, never the payoff and never the killing: an adult woman in the scale hall offers one identifiable heart in a gold dish, and Ammit's receiving hands are the focal point beneath the pan. Two figures are present, but Ammit's crouch and the giver's arm stay readable. Face and eye line sit around y 390, with the dish, hands, and pan in the central band.
- **Costume & attire:** Minimal mythic hunter's harness and layered sandstone-and-gold shoulder wrap built around the crocodile jaw and lion mane silhouette; no generic red cloth. Her adult body is broad through the shoulders and hips, with thick forearms and a low crouched center of gravity.
- **Palette:** B/R identity anchor: B `#5a3a70`, `#140d1c`, `#9b6fc4` layered with R `#d95436`, `#5e0f0f`, `#f7b267`; Duat accents tomb gold `#e8c95a` / `#c9a84c`, lapis `#1f3f7a` / `#2f5fae`, sandstone `#d8b98a` / `#a8834f`, resin brown `#4a2f16`, and reserved heart-red `#c8253a` only on the single weighed heart in the gold dish and no other surface. Value floor: no true black; darkest visible value is resin brown `#3a2a1c`.
- **Lighting:** Named practical key: a low gold oil lamp inside the judgment hall, catching Ammit's hands, crocodile jaw, and wet mouth. One lapis-cool rim from the scale's blue inlay and the shadowed pan; no extra key.
- **Expression:** Eyes narrowed with calm hunger, mouth open only enough to reveal a natural dark mouth interior, never red; no gore, no grin, no institutional caricature.
- **Props / weapon:** The offered heart in a gold dish is already out of the giver's reach; Ammit's hands receive it. The empty pan above her and Anubis's distant level beam frame the payment. The reward and the killing remain off-frame.
- **Background:** Resin-brown alabaster hall beneath the scale, with a gold beam receding into lapis-edged colonnade and the giver's adult silhouette kept secondary. Every cartouche and wall register is a blank gold or lapis panel with geometric banding, never glyphs.
- **Holo interaction:** Shiny highlight catches the lamp-gold hands and scale edge while heart-red remains a controlled, isolated hue.
- **Rarity ambition:** UR legend intent: high-contrast predator silhouette and a readable Rite payment at thumbnail size, with restraint replacing gore. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q4):** the heart-red reservation is confirmed and binding.

### Neith, Who Weaves the Shroud — `sd-neith-who-weaves-the-shroud`
- **Card facts:** {3}{U}{W} · W/U (gold frame) · 4/4 · ssr, legendary · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** Adult woman and named Neith legend, a weaver-huntress whose shroud, bowstring, and river channel are one craft. She is lean, long-limbed, and athletic with warm bronze skin and an adult hunter's poise. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q1):** Anuket joins as the third blue legend; cast balance settled.
- **Personality / mood:** Exacting, patient, and quietly inventive; everything is made to be used twice. Her calm is craft discipline, not domestic softness.
- **Pose & composition:** Preserve copy is the primary read: the original figure is low in resin-brown shadow at the loom while an identical token copy stands in clean natron white and gold in the space the original occupied. Retell is shown by the shuttle's repeated path and a second clean length of woven linen echoing the first, not by text. Face and eye line around y 310; loom, copy, and reed channel occupy the safe central band.
- **Costume & attire:** Adult weaver-huntress in a fitted linen wrap with a bare forearm for the shuttle hand, lapis beadwork, gold shoulder clasp, and a hunting bow slung across her back. Low braided hair leaves generous headroom; no towering crown.
- **Palette:** U/W identity anchor: U `#4a90d9`, `#16294f`, `#a8d4f7` layered with W `#f2e8cf`, `#c9a84c`, `#fffef2`; Duat accents tomb gold `#e8c95a` / `#c9a84c`, lapis `#1f3f7a` / `#2f5fae`, sandstone `#d8b98a` / `#a8834f`, natron white `#f4efe2`, and resin brown `#4a2f16`. Heart-red `#c8253a` is absent. Value floor: no true black; darkest visible value is resin brown `#3a2a1c`.
- **Lighting:** Named practical key: a warm oil lamp mounted beside the vertical loom, lighting the natron copy, shuttle, and face. One lapis-cool rim from the river channel and wet reeds; no second key.
- **Expression:** Focused adult concentration, eyes following the shuttle's return path; a small confident lift at one brow suggests the maker already knows the work will hold.
- **Props / weapon:** Shuttle in the original's hand, identical shuttle in the copy's hand, half-woven natron linen, and bow slung securely at the back. The duplicated craft is the Retell echo; the bright copy is the Preserve signal.
- **Background:** Fully rendered loom straddling a reed channel with lapis water, sandstone bank, layered reeds, and a receding path of blank geometric panels. Every wall register, cartouche, and cloth band is blank or geometric, never written.
- **Holo interaction:** Shiny highlight may animate lapis water-light across both silhouettes and the clean natron copy.
- **Rarity ambition:** SSR legend intent: rich craft environment, double silhouette, and a crisp copy-versus-original contrast that survives the battlefield crop. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q1):** Anuket joins as the third blue legend; cast balance settled.
- **Prompt:** Adult woman Neith, lean athletic weaver-huntress at a vertical loom strung across a reed channel, Preserve composition with an original low in resin-brown shadow and an identical clean token copy standing in natron white and gold in the same space, Retell shown by two shuttle passes and a second length of woven linen, bow slung at her back, focused bronze adult face around y 310, U/W identity palette with Duat tomb gold, lapis, sandstone, natron white, resin brown, warm oil lamp beside the loom as practical key and one lapis-cool river rim, blank gold cartouche panels, geometric banded registers, NO-TEXT, no hieroglyphs, no glyphs, no writing, no inscriptions, no true black, darkest visible value resin brown `#3a2a1c`, generous headroom, fully rendered reeds and water background — crisp cel-shaded gacha anime splash art, fully rendered scenic background, 640×800 portrait

### Ra, Helm of the Night Barge — `sd-ra-helm-of-the-night-barge`
- **Card facts:** {5}{R}{W} · R/W (gold frame) · 5/5 · skyborne, sentinel · ssr, legendary · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** Adult woman and named Ra legend, the sun-barge helmswoman who makes the night crossing twelve hours at a time. She is tall, muscular, and broad-backed from rowing, with deep bronze skin and a disciplined adult stance.
- **Personality / mood:** Habit has worn fear into endurance. She ignores the serpent because the crossing must continue, not because the danger is small.
- **Pose & composition:** Empower is shown as force carried through action: Ra leans into the steering oar while the gilded barge surges forward and a resin-brown serpent coil breaks the water ahead without becoming the subject. A ring of barge-fire motes strengthens along the prow. Face and eye line around y 300; oar, prow fire, and wake occupy the central band.
- **Costume & attire:** Sun-disc crown kept low enough for headroom, layered gold-and-linen helmswoman harness, pleated wrap, bronze bracers, and a broad adult athletic frame. Her shoulders and oar form the hero silhouette; no extra crew member competes with her.
- **Palette:** R/W identity anchor: R `#d95436`, `#5e0f0f`, `#f7b267` layered with W `#f2e8cf`, `#c9a84c`, `#fffef2`; Duat accents tomb gold `#e8c95a` / `#c9a84c`, lapis `#1f3f7a` / `#2f5fae`, sandstone `#d8b98a` / `#a8834f`, barge fire `#ff8a3d` / `#ffd07a`, and resin brown `#4a2f16`. Heart-red `#c8253a` is absent; red identity uses barge fire only. Value floor: no true black; darkest visible value is resin brown `#3a2a1c`.
- **Lighting:** Named practical key: the visible barge prow-fire below and ahead of her, lighting the oar, hands, and lower face. One lapis-cool rim from the night river and sky; no true-black void and no second key.
- **Expression:** Focused, tired, and utterly steady adult resolve; she looks past the serpent toward the channel.
- **Props / weapon:** Steering oar, gilded prow fittings, rising barge-fire ring, and the serpent's reflected coil. The oar must have correct hand placement and remain separate from the hull.
- **Background:** Fully rendered night channel with layered sandstone narrows, lapis water, a burning wake, and the serpent coil breaking the surface ahead. Blank gold cartouche panels and geometric banded registers appear on the barge fittings, never glyphs.
- **Holo interaction:** Shiny highlight catches the warm prow-fire and sun disc while lapis rim preserves the silhouette.
- **Rarity ambition:** UR legend intent: kinetic but composed helm action, a bright central fire read, and a mythic night crossing without a black rectangle.

### Renenutet, Who Measures the Flood — `sd-renenutet-who-measures-the-flood`
- **Card facts:** {5}{G} · G · 5/5 · wardingGaze · ssr, legendary · holo: shiny
- **Character & source:** Adult woman and named Renenutet legend, the cobra-hooded harvest goddess who sets the flood height. She is full-figured, tall, and grounded with warm brown skin, strong arms, and an adult agrarian authority. **Ruled 2026-08-18 (Q6):** the roughly one-third daylight share stands.
- **Personality / mood:** Deliberate and withholding; the delta learns her decision from the water line instead of a proclamation. The mood is fertile power under an unanswerable sky.
- **Pose & composition:** Empower ramp is shown by the measured release of land: one palm is flat on the flood-gauge stone at this year's line while two newly opened water channels feed green planting beds behind her. The gauge, waterline, and paired channels sit in the central band; face and eye line around y 335.
